== CITE PrintedPolarizationDictionary.lean:72
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32Native/PrintedPolarizationDictionary.lean  (lines 70–97)
   70| The geometric action induces `P.W`; the polarization class is constructed from `P.E`.
   71| The two named comparison steps above retain their separate proof obligations. -/
   72| theorem printed_polarization_dictionary (P : PrintedPolarizedTriple A)
   73|     (hdisc : P.discriminant = classNegTwo) :
   74|     ∃ pol : HQ A 2,
   75|       IsMemberOfM (finiteBetti_abelian A) (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A)
   76|         (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A) (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) P.W pol := by
   77|   letI := (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A).finite
   78|   obtain ⟨pol, hω, hR, hpos, hform⟩ := printed_polarization_reconstruction_aux P
   79|   refine ⟨pol, ⟨P.dim_eq, printed_tangent_weil_type_aux P, hω, hR, hpos, ?_⟩⟩
   80|   let J : {J : Module.End ℚ (HQ A 1) // J ∘ₗ J = (-3 : ℚ) • LinearMap.id} :=
   81|     ⟨P.W.J, P.W.J_sq⟩
   82|   have heven : Even (finrank (QuadraticAlgebra ℚ (-3) 0) (WithJ (HQ A 1) J)) := by
   83|     have hf := finrank_withJ J
   84|     rw [(torusCohomology_cupProductOf A).finrank_eq, P.dim_eq] at hf
   85|     have h6 : finrank (QuadraticAlgebra ℚ (-3) 0) (WithJ (HQ A 1) J) = 6 := by omega
   86|     rw [h6]
   87|     exact ⟨3, rfl⟩
   88|   have hE : (riemannForm (polarizationVec (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) pol)).IsAlt :=
   89|     hform.symm ▸ P.alternating
   90|   have hn : (riemannForm (polarizationVec (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) pol)).Nondegenerate :=
   91|     hform.symm ▸ P.nondegenerate
   92|   have hd := polarization_dual_determinant J hω hR hE hn heven
   93|   have hh : P.discriminant =
   94|       weilDetClass (riemannForm (polarizationVec (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) pol))
   95|         (transposeAction J) (riemannForm_skew J hR) hE hn := by
   96|     simp only [PrintedPolarizedTriple.discriminant, hform, J]
   97|   exact hd.symm.trans (hh.symm.trans hdisc)

== CITE RealDefs/AbelianVariety.lean:354–365
FILE HodgeLean/RealDefs/AbelianVariety.lean  (lines 352–368)
  352| `cupWedgeMap` to be a morphism of Hodge structures (from O5, `CupFiltered`). It is proved, under
  353| C1, C2, C4, C7 and O5, as `isExteriorOfWeightOne_of_cupFiltered` in `AbelianExterior.lean`. -/
  354| noncomputable def abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (hF : FiniteBetti X) (hB : BettiDeRhamIso X)
  355|     (hD : HodgeDecomposition X) (hT : TorusCohomology μ) :
  356|     letI := hT.finite
  357|     HodgeBlocks.AbelianVarietyCohomology (HQ X 1) :=
  358|   letI := hT.finite
  359|   { g := dim X.left
  360|     rank_eq := hT.finrank_eq
  361|     weight1 := cohomologyHodgeStructure hF hB hD 1
  362|     cohomology := fun m =>
  363|       letI := hF.finite m
  364|       HodgeBlocks.QHodgeStructure.congr (HQ X m) (cohomologyHodgeStructure hF hB hD m)
  365|         (hT.cupWedgeEquiv m).symm }
  367| /-- A class `v ∈ ⋀^(2p) H¹` is a Hodge class of the package's `cohomology (2p)` exactly when its
  368| cup product `cupWedgeMap (2p) v` lies in upstream's `Hdg^p(X; ℚ)`. -/

   73| Relation to the source.
   74| * **Translation.** Kollár's "projective morphism" [Kol07, (2) and (3); Theorem 27] is
   75|   `_root_.HodgeLean.Ported.Hironaka.IsProjective`, Grothendieck's definition as in [Sta, Tag 01W8]: `E` ranges over the
   76|   quasi-coherent `𝒪_Y`-modules of finite type (Mathlib's `SheafOfModules.IsQuasicoherent` and
   77|   `SheafOfModules.IsFiniteType`). Kollár's varieties are quasi-projective, and over a base with an
   78|   ample invertible sheaf this is equivalent to being a closed subscheme of some `ℙⁿ_Y`
   79|   [Sta, Tag 087S]; for a general base the condition with `ℙⁿ_Y` is stronger, and the
   80|   condition on an open cover of `Y` only ("locally projective") is weaker [Sta, Tag 01W8], a
   81|   distinction Kollár draws in [Kol07, Example 28.1]. -/
   82| class _root_.HodgeLean.Ported.Hironaka.IsProjective {X Y : Scheme.{u}} (f : X ⟶ Y) : Prop where
   83|   exists_isClosedImmersion : ∃ (E : Y.Modules) (_ : E.IsQuasicoherent)
   84|     (_ : SheafOfModules.IsFiniteType.{u} E) (i : X ⟶ E.projectiveBundle),
   85|     IsClosedImmersion i ∧ i ≫ E.projectiveBundleπ = f

== CITE algebraicCycleClassSpan -> AlgebraicGeometry.ComplexPoint.algebraicCycleClassSpan
FILE .lake/packages/HodgeConjecture/HodgeConjecture/Definitions/AlgebraicGeometry/Cycle/FundamentalClass.lean  (lines 94–101)
   94| /-- Let `X` be a smooth integral projective scheme over `ℂ` and `p` a natural number. This is the
   95| rational subspace of `H^{2p}(X(ℂ); ℚ)` spanned by the fundamental cohomology classes of all
   96| codimension-`p` integral closed subvarieties of `X`. Their classes are normalized by the complex
   97| orientations of their normal spaces on their smooth loci. -/

   99|   sSup {Submodule.span ℚ {cycleComponentSheafClass X x hx} |
  100|     (x : X.left) (hx : Order.coheight x = p) }

== CITE AbelianPowerData -> HodgeLean.Adapters.AbelianPowerData
FILE HodgeLean/Adapters/AbelianCMDataDefs.lean  (lines 127–155)
  127| /-- **Power data**: an abelian `Y` with `r` group homomorphisms `p_i : Y ⟶ A` and `s_i : A ⟶ Y`
  128| satisfying the biproduct identities `p_j ∘ s_i = δ_ij` (`1` is the zero homomorphism, the
  129| constant map to the identity point) and `Σ_i s_i ∘ p_i = 𝟙 Y` (sum in the group `Y ⟶ Y`). The
  130| power `A^r` with its projections and inclusions is the intended instance; it is not built here.
  131| Source: PW v0.3, Theorem 4.2 (the powers `A^r`); Mac Lane, *Categories for the Working
  132| Mathematician*, §VIII.2 (biproducts in additive categories); Mumford, *Abelian Varieties*, §19
  133| (the category of abelian varieties is additive). -/
  134| structure AbelianPowerData (A Y : Over (Spec ↧ℂ)) [GrpObj A] [GrpObj Y] [IsCommMonObj Y]
  135|     (r : ℕ) where
  136|   /-- The projections `p_i : Y ⟶ A`. -/
  137|   proj : Fin r → (Y ⟶ A)
  138|   /-- The inclusions `s_i : A ⟶ Y`. -/
  139|   incl : Fin r → (A ⟶ Y)
  140|   proj_isMonHom : ∀ i, IsMonHom (proj i)
  141|   incl_isMonHom : ∀ i, IsMonHom (incl i)
  142|   /-- `p_j ∘ s_i = δ_ij`. -/
  143|   incl_proj : ∀ i j, incl i ≫ proj j = if i = j then 𝟙 A else 1
  144|   /-- `Σ_i s_i ∘ p_i = 𝟙 Y`. -/
  145|   sum_proj_incl : ∏ i, (proj i ≫ incl i) = 𝟙 Y
  147| /-- The first power: `Y = A`, `p = s = 𝟙 A`. -/
  148| example : AbelianPowerData A A 1 where
  149|   proj _ := 𝟙 A
  150|   incl _ := 𝟙 A
  151|   proj_isMonHom _ := inferInstance
  152|   incl_isMonHom _ := inferInstance
  153|   incl_proj i j := by rw [Subsingleton.elim i j, ite_eq_left_iff.2 fun h => (h rfl).elim, Category.comp_id]
  154|   sum_proj_incl := by rw [Fin.prod_univ_one, Category.comp_id]

   30| /-- Let `X` be an irreducible topological space. Its dimension is the supremum of the lengths of
   31| strict chains of nonempty irreducible closed subsets, converted to a natural number. A chain of

== CITE HodgeBlocks.WeilAction -> HodgeBlocks.WeilAction

   25| /-- The economical datum needed from a `K`-action: the image `J` of the chosen square root,
   26| its quadratic relation, and preservation of every Hodge piece.  A full `Module K V` is not

  136|     (∀ x, homPullback f 1 (Q.W.J x) = P.W.J (homPullback f 1 x)) ∧
  137|     ∃ c : ℚ, 0 < c ∧ homPullback f 2 Q.pol = c • P.pol
  139| /-- The intertwining clause for `J_Q` and `J_P` gives the same clause for the conjugate actions
  140| `-J_Q` and `-J_P`. -/
  141| example (P Q : g32t_Sixfold) (f : P.X ⟶ Q.X) :
  142|     letI := (torusCohomology_cupProductOf P.X).finite
  143|     letI := (torusCohomology_cupProductOf Q.X).finite
  144|     (∀ x, homPullback f 1 (Q.W.J x) = P.W.J (homPullback f 1 x)) →
  145|       ∀ x, homPullback f 1 (-Q.W.J x) = -P.W.J (homPullback f 1 x) := by
  146|   intro h x
  147|   rw [map_neg, h]
  149| /-- **The transfer datum of G32 Theorem 7.2.** A family of sixfolds over an index set `S` (the
  150| Hurwitz component `Hur°`), the nine-dimensional moduli space `M = Γ(ℓ)\𝓗₃` as a connected
  151| Hausdorff analytic complex manifold, and its universal family `univ`, with the outputs of the
  152| earlier stages and the literature premises as fields:
  154| * `fiber_polarized` (stage 2, G32 Lemma 3.6): each fibre is a polarized sixfold of Weil type;
  155| * (c) `fiber_disc` (stage 5, G32 Proposition 6.3): each fibre has discriminant `[-2]`;
  156| * (a) `fiber_alg` (stage 3, G32 Proposition 4.6, from Schoen and Milne): the Weil classes of
  157|   each fibre are algebraic;
  158| * (b) `dominant` (stage 4, G32 Corollary 5.11 and the first paragraph of the proof of Theorem
  159|   7.2, from the rank-nine certificate): a nonempty open `U ⊆ M` each of whose points carries a
  160|   fibre of `univ` receiving a `K`-linear polarized isogeny from some fibre of the family;
  161| * (d) `spread` (G32 Lemma 7.1, Voisin's relative-Hilbert argument): the points of `M` where the
  162|   Weil classes of the universal fibre are algebraic form a countable union of closed analytic
  163|   subsets (closed algebraic subsets of the quasi-projective `M` are closed analytic);
  164| * (e) `cover` (G32 Lemma 2.2, second sentence, from Landherr's theorem and van Geemen §5):
  165|   every member of `M = (3, ℚ(√-3), [-2])` has a `K`-linear polarized isogeny to a fibre of
  166|   `univ`;
  167| * (f) `transport` (G32 Lemma 2.2, first sentence): algebraicity of the Weil classes is invariant
  168|   under `K`-linear polarized isogeny.
  170| The family structure of `fiber` and `univ` (an abelian scheme over a base) is not recorded: the
  171| repository has no families of abelian schemes. The fields use the fibres only pointwise, and
  172| the family structure enters through the stages that supply (b) and (d).
  173| Source: G32 v0.16, Section 2.1 (the moduli space `𝓜` and its universal abelian scheme), Lemma
  174| 2.2, Lemma 7.1, Theorem 7.2 and the proof of Theorem A; van Geemen, *An introduction to the Hodge
  175| conjecture for abelian varieties*, LNM 1594 (1994), §§5.2–5.11; Voisin, *Hodge Theory and Complex
  176| Algebraic Geometry II*, §3.3.2. -/

== CITE homPullback -> HodgeLean.Adapters.homPullback
FILE HodgeLean/Adapters/AbelianHomPullbackDefs.lean  (lines 27–43)
   27| /-- Pullback along the map of complex points of `f : Y ⟶ X`, on singular cohomology, carried from
   28| `X(ℂ)` to `HQ X n` and from `Y(ℂ)` to `HQ Y n` along the S7 comparisons.
   29| Source: Hatcher, *Algebraic Topology*, §3.1 (induced maps on cohomology); Birkenhake–Lange,
   30| *Complex Abelian Varieties*, 2nd ed., §1.2 (rational representation of a homomorphism); upstream
   31| `Point.continuousMap` at `2ead713`; S7 `cupComparison`. -/
   32| def homPullback (f : Y ⟶ X) (n : ℕ) : HQ X n →ₗ[ℚ] HQ Y n :=
   33|   (cupComparison Y n).symm.toLinearMap ∘ₗ
   34|     cohomologyMap ℚ n (TopCat.ofHom (Point.continuousMap f)) ∘ₗ (cupComparison X n).toLinearMap
   36| example (f : Y ⟶ X) (n : ℕ) (x : HQ X n) :
   37|     cupComparison Y n (homPullback f n x) =
   38|       cohomologyMap ℚ n (TopCat.ofHom (Point.continuousMap f)) (cupComparison X n x) := by
   39|   simp [homPullback]
   41| -- For an endomorphism it is the existing endomorphism pullback.
   42| example (f : X ⟶ X) (n : ℕ) : homPullback f n = complexPointPullback X f n := rfl

   50| /-- Printed geometric input in universal-cover tangent coordinates and rational homology
   51| coordinates. Homology is represented by the dual of `HQ A 1`, with its actual period lattice;
   52| the real complex structure is the transpose of the canonical structure on cohomology.

   56| `omega` in `E(x,Jy) + omega * E(x,y)`, as in `WeilHermitian.weilHermitian`. -/

   62|   tangent_minus : finrank ℂ (Module.End.eigenspace

   73|       (y ∘ₗ weightOneComplexStructure A (finiteBetti_abelian A) (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A)) =

   76|     0 < printedRealForm A E
   77|       (x ∘ₗ weightOneComplexStructure A (finiteBetti_abelian A) (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A)) x
   78|   integral_scale : ∃ c : ℚ, 0 < c ∧ ∀ l m : abelianPeriodLattice A,
   79|     ∃ z : ℤ, c * E (printedLatticeVector A l) (printedLatticeVector A m) = z

   72| Consistency and non-vacuity: at `R = ℚ[J]`, `J² = -3`, it is equivalent to the hypothesis `hSU`

== CITE G32Native.TypeIIISixfoldData -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.G32Native.TypeIIISixfoldData

   16| /-- A diagonal quaternionic homology presentation of the polarized variety in
   17| Lemma 8.1. The action is the actual rational endomorphism pullback, retained on
   18| integral endomorphisms. The trace equation is on homology, the dual of `HQ A 1`.
   19| No Weil-type or discriminant comparison is a field of these data.
   20| Source: G32 v0.16, section 8, paragraph before Lemma 8.1;
   21| Birkenhake–Lange, Complex Abelian Varieties, second edition, sections 5.2, 9.4–9.6. -/
   22| structure TypeIIISixfoldData (A : Over (Spec ↧ℂ))
   23|     [GrpObj A] [IsCommMonObj A] [IsIntegral A.left] [Smooth A.hom]
   24|     [IsProjective A.hom] (pol : HQ A 2) where
   25|   b : ℚ
   26|   b_neg : b < 0
   27|   endomorphisms : ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b] ≃ₐ[ℚ] AbelianEnd0 A
   28|   pullback : AbelianEnd0 A →ₐ[ℚ] (Module.End ℚ (HQ A 1))ᵐᵒᵖ
   29|   pullback_integral : ∀ f, pullback (1 ⊗ₜ f) = MulOpposite.op (abelianEndAction A f)
   30|   homology : Module.Dual ℚ (HQ A 1) ≃ₗ[ℚ] (Fin 3 → ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b])
   31|   homology_action : ∀ q x i,
   32|     homology (((pullback (endomorphisms q)).unop).dualMap x) i = q * homology x i
   33|   l1 : Fin 3 → ℚ
   34|   l2 : Fin 3 → ℚ
   35|   l3 : Fin 3 → ℚ
   36|   diagonal_nonzero : ∀ i, (-3 * l1 i ^ 2 + b * l2 i ^ 2 + 3 * b * l3 i ^ 2) ≠ 0
   37|   nondegenerate : IsNondegenerate (polarizationVec (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) pol)
   38|   positive : PolarizationPositive (finiteBetti_abelian A) (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A)
   39|     (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A) (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) nondegenerate
   40|   trace_eq : ∀ x y,
   41|     y (contract (polarizationVec (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) pol) x) =
   42|       2 * (∑ i, homology x i *
   43|         (⟨0, l1 i, l2 i, l3 i⟩ : ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b]) * star (homology y i)).re
   46| variable {A : Over (Spec ↧ℂ)} [GrpObj A] [IsCommMonObj A]
   47|   [IsIntegral A.left] [Smooth A.hom] [IsProjective A.hom] {pol : HQ A 2}

== CITE TypeIIIFormData.diagonal -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.G32Native.TypeIIIFormData.diagonal
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32Native/TypeIIIFormDiagonalization.lean  (lines 44–102)
   44| /-- Diagonalize the supplied form using its selected orthogonal basis.
   45| Source: G32 v0.18, section 8, lines 688–689 (quaternionic Gram–Schmidt). -/
   46| def TypeIIIFormData.diagonal (F : TypeIIIFormData A pol) : TypeIIISixfoldData A pol := by
   47|   classical
   48|   letI := typeIIIQuaternionDivisionRing F.b F.b_neg
   49|   have hex := typeIII_skew_orthogonal_basis F.b F.b_neg 3 F.T
   50|     F.add_left F.add_right F.smul_left F.smul_right F.skew F.regular
   51|     (by simp [Module.finrank_pi_fintype])
   52|   let eD := Classical.choose hex
   53|   have horth := (Classical.choose_spec hex).1
   54|   have hdiag := (Classical.choose_spec hex).2
   55|   let e := F.coordinates.trans (eD.equivFun.restrictScalars ℚ)
   56|   let t := fun i => F.T (eD i) (eD i)
   57|   have ht (i) : (t i).re = 0 := by
   58|     have h := congrArg QuaternionAlgebra.re (F.skew (eD i) (eD i))
   59|     simp only [QuaternionAlgebra.re_neg, QuaternionAlgebra.re_star, zero_mul, add_zero] at h
   60|     change (t i).re = -(t i).re at h
   61|     linarith
   62|   let l1 := fun i => (t i).imI
   63|   let l2 := fun i => (t i).imJ
   64|   let l3 := fun i => (t i).imK
   65|   have htEntry (i) : (⟨0, l1 i, l2 i, l3 i⟩ : ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, F.b]) = t i := by
   66|     ext
   67|     · exact (ht i).symm
   68|     · rfl
   69|     · rfl
   70|     · rfl
   71|   refine { b := F.b, b_neg := F.b_neg, endomorphisms := F.endomorphisms
   72|            pullback := abelianEnd0Action A, pullback_integral := ?_
   73|            homology := e, homology_action := ?_
   74|            l1 := l1, l2 := l2, l3 := l3, diagonal_nonzero := ?_
   75|            nondegenerate := F.nondegenerate, positive := F.positive, trace_eq := ?_ }
   76|   · intro f
   77|     simpa only [one_smul] using spec_g4_abelianEnd0Action_tmul A 1 f
   78|   · intro q x i
   79|     have heq : F.coordinates (((abelianEnd0Action A (F.endomorphisms q)).unop).dualMap x) =
   80|         q • F.coordinates x := by
   81|       funext j
   82|       exact F.action q x j
   83|     change eD.equivFun (F.coordinates (((abelianEnd0Action A (F.endomorphisms q)).unop).dualMap x)) i = _
   84|     rw [heq, map_smul]
   85|     rfl
   86|   · intro i hz
   87|     apply hdiag i
   88|     apply (typeIIIQuaternionNorm_eq_zero F.b F.b_neg (t i)).1
   89|     have hz' : -3 * (t i).imI ^ 2 + F.b * (t i).imJ ^ 2 + 3 * F.b * (t i).imK ^ 2 = 0 := hz
   90|     change (t i).re ^ 2 + 3 * (t i).imI ^ 2 - F.b * (t i).imJ ^ 2 -
   91|       3 * F.b * (t i).imK ^ 2 = 0
   92|     rw [ht]
   93|     linarith
   94|   · intro x y
   95|     rw [F.trace]
   96|     have hexp := typeIII_skew_basis_expansion F.T F.add_left F.add_right
   97|       F.smul_left F.smul_right eD horth (F.coordinates x) (F.coordinates y)
   98|     rw [hexp]
   99|     simp_rw [htEntry]
  100|     rfl
  102| /-- The quaternion parameter is retained. -/

== CITE TypeIIIFormData -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.G32Native.TypeIIIFormData
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32Native/TypeIIIFormDiagonalization.lean  (lines 14–45)
   14| /-- A skew-Hermitian form on quaternionic homology with its reduced trace
   15| polarization. Coordinates here need not be orthogonal.
   16| Source: G32 v0.18, section 8, line 689 and the preceding paragraph. -/
   17| structure TypeIIIFormData (A : Over (Spec ↧ℂ))
   18|     [GrpObj A] [IsCommMonObj A] [IsIntegral A.left] [Smooth A.hom]
   19|     [IsProjective A.hom] (pol : HQ A 2) where
   20|   b : ℚ
   21|   b_neg : b < 0
   22|   endomorphisms : ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b] ≃ₐ[ℚ] AbelianEnd0 A
   23|   coordinates : Dual ℚ (HQ A 1) ≃ₗ[ℚ] (Fin 3 → ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b])
   24|   action : ∀ q x i,
   25|     coordinates (((abelianEnd0Action A (endomorphisms q)).unop).dualMap x) i =
   26|       q * coordinates x i
   27|   T : (Fin 3 → ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b]) → (Fin 3 → ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b]) → ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b]
   28|   add_left : ∀ x y z, T (x + y) z = T x z + T y z
   29|   add_right : ∀ x y z, T x (y + z) = T x y + T x z
   30|   smul_left : ∀ (q : ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b]) x y, T (q • x) y = q * T x y
   31|   smul_right : ∀ (q : ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b]) x y, T x (q • y) = T x y * star q
   32|   skew : ∀ x y, T y x = -star (T x y)
   33|   regular : ∀ x, (∀ y, T x y = 0) → x = 0
   34|   nondegenerate : IsNondegenerate (polarizationVec (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) pol)
   35|   positive : PolarizationPositive (finiteBetti_abelian A) (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A)
   36|     (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A) (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) nondegenerate
   37|   trace : ∀ x y,
   38|     y (contract (polarizationVec (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) pol) x) =
   39|       2 * (T (coordinates x) (coordinates y)).re
   41| variable {A : Over (Spec ↧ℂ)} [GrpObj A] [IsCommMonObj A]
   42|   [IsIntegral A.left] [Smooth A.hom] [IsProjective A.hom] {pol : HQ A 2}
   44| /-- Diagonalize the supplied form using its selected orthogonal basis.
   45| Source: G32 v0.18, section 8, lines 688–689 (quaternionic Gram–Schmidt). -/

   37| /-- The class of −b disc(T), defined from the trace polarization and the

   39| The diagonalization supplies only the square and skewness certificates. -/

== CITE TypeIIIFormData.diagonal -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.G32Native.TypeIIIFormData.diagonal
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32Native/TypeIIIFormDiagonalization.lean  (lines 44–102)
   44| /-- Diagonalize the supplied form using its selected orthogonal basis.
   45| Source: G32 v0.18, section 8, lines 688–689 (quaternionic Gram–Schmidt). -/
   46| def TypeIIIFormData.diagonal (F : TypeIIIFormData A pol) : TypeIIISixfoldData A pol := by
   47|   classical
   48|   letI := typeIIIQuaternionDivisionRing F.b F.b_neg
   49|   have hex := typeIII_skew_orthogonal_basis F.b F.b_neg 3 F.T
   50|     F.add_left F.add_right F.smul_left F.smul_right F.skew F.regular
   51|     (by simp [Module.finrank_pi_fintype])
   52|   let eD := Classical.choose hex
   53|   have horth := (Classical.choose_spec hex).1
   54|   have hdiag := (Classical.choose_spec hex).2
   55|   let e := F.coordinates.trans (eD.equivFun.restrictScalars ℚ)
   56|   let t := fun i => F.T (eD i) (eD i)
   57|   have ht (i) : (t i).re = 0 := by
   58|     have h := congrArg QuaternionAlgebra.re (F.skew (eD i) (eD i))
   59|     simp only [QuaternionAlgebra.re_neg, QuaternionAlgebra.re_star, zero_mul, add_zero] at h
   60|     change (t i).re = -(t i).re at h
   61|     linarith
   62|   let l1 := fun i => (t i).imI
   63|   let l2 := fun i => (t i).imJ
   64|   let l3 := fun i => (t i).imK
   65|   have htEntry (i) : (⟨0, l1 i, l2 i, l3 i⟩ : ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, F.b]) = t i := by
   66|     ext
   67|     · exact (ht i).symm
   68|     · rfl
   69|     · rfl
   70|     · rfl
   71|   refine { b := F.b, b_neg := F.b_neg, endomorphisms := F.endomorphisms
   72|            pullback := abelianEnd0Action A, pullback_integral := ?_
   73|            homology := e, homology_action := ?_
   74|            l1 := l1, l2 := l2, l3 := l3, diagonal_nonzero := ?_
   75|            nondegenerate := F.nondegenerate, positive := F.positive, trace_eq := ?_ }
   76|   · intro f
   77|     simpa only [one_smul] using spec_g4_abelianEnd0Action_tmul A 1 f
   78|   · intro q x i
   79|     have heq : F.coordinates (((abelianEnd0Action A (F.endomorphisms q)).unop).dualMap x) =
   80|         q • F.coordinates x := by
   81|       funext j
   82|       exact F.action q x j
   83|     change eD.equivFun (F.coordinates (((abelianEnd0Action A (F.endomorphisms q)).unop).dualMap x)) i = _
   84|     rw [heq, map_smul]
   85|     rfl
   86|   · intro i hz
   87|     apply hdiag i
   88|     apply (typeIIIQuaternionNorm_eq_zero F.b F.b_neg (t i)).1
   89|     have hz' : -3 * (t i).imI ^ 2 + F.b * (t i).imJ ^ 2 + 3 * F.b * (t i).imK ^ 2 = 0 := hz
   90|     change (t i).re ^ 2 + 3 * (t i).imI ^ 2 - F.b * (t i).imJ ^ 2 -
   91|       3 * F.b * (t i).imK ^ 2 = 0
   92|     rw [ht]
   93|     linarith
   94|   · intro x y
   95|     rw [F.trace]
   96|     have hexp := typeIII_skew_basis_expansion F.T F.add_left F.add_right
   97|       F.smul_left F.smul_right eD horth (F.coordinates x) (F.coordinates y)
   98|     rw [hexp]
   99|     simp_rw [htEntry]
  100|     rfl
  102| /-- The quaternion parameter is retained. -/

== CITE PELSplitFamily -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.GeneralTypeIII.PELSplitFamily
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32Native/PELSplitFamilyData.lean  (lines 12–54)
   12| /-- An algebraic realization of the entire retained period component on the
   13| original reference lattice. The total space is a commutative group over a
   14| connected base; the integral order operators extend over that base. The
   15| period and integral marking equations specify the fibres. No Hodge-group
   16| comparison or exactness of the fibre End⁰ action is a field.
   17| Source: Abdulali, Annals 155 (2002), §§2.2–2.3;
   18| Shimura, On analytic families of polarized abelian varieties and automorphic
   19| functions, Annals 78 (1963), Propositions 15 and 17; Birkenhake–Lange, §9.6. -/
   20| structure PELSplitFamily (R : PELSplitReference) where
   21|   base : Over (Spec ↧ℂ)
   22|   connected : _root_.IsConnected (Set.univ : Set base.left)
   23|   abelian : RelativeAbelianScheme base.left
   24|   projective : IsProjective abelian.total.hom
   25|   point : PELSplitComponent R → (Over.mk (𝟙 (Spec ↧ℂ)) ⟶ base)
   26|   onto : Function.Surjective point
   27|   fibre : PELSplitComponent R → wtr_Variety 3
   28|   polarized : ∀ z, wtr_PolarizedWeil 3 (fibre z)
   29|   fibreIso : ∀ z, (fibre z).X ≅ (Over.pullback (point z).left).obj abelian.total
   30|   mark : ∀ z, HQ R.B.X 1 ≃ₗ[ℚ] HQ (fibre z).X 1
   31|   integralMark : ∀ z, abelianPeriodLattice R.B.X ≃+ abelianPeriodLattice (fibre z).X
   32|   period_eq : ∀ z x l,
   33|     abelianPeriod (fibre z).X (mark z x) (integralMark z l) = abelianPeriod R.B.X x l
   34|   plane_eq : ∀ z, (pelSplitPlane R z.val).map ((mark z).toLinearMap.baseChange ℂ) =
   35|     complexHolomorphicH1 (fibre z).X
   36|   form_eq : ∀ z x y,
   37|     polarizationForm (torusCohomology_cupProductOf (fibre z).X)
   38|       (polarized z).nondegenerate (mark z x) (mark z y) =
   39|     polarizationForm (torusCohomology_cupProductOf R.B.X) R.polarized.nondegenerate x y
   40|   generator_eq : ∀ z x, mark z (R.B.W.J x) = (fibre z).W.J (mark z x)
   41|   action : ∀ z, ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, R.b] →ₐ[ℚ] AbelianEnd0 (fibre z).X
   42|   action_eq : ∀ z d x,
   43|     (abelianEnd0Action (fibre z).X (action z d)).unop (mark z x) =
   44|       mark z ((abelianEnd0Action R.B.X (R.end0 d)).unop x)
   45|   integral : ∀ z, R.order →+* AbelianEnd (fibre z).X
   46|   integral_eq : ∀ z a, (1 : ℚ) ⊗ₜ[ℤ] integral z a = action z a.val
   47|   integral_transport : ∀ z a l,
   48|     geometricIntegralOperator (fibre z).X (integral z a) (integralMark z l) =
   49|       integralMark z (R.integralAction a l)
   50|   orderMap : R.order → (abelian.total ⟶ abelian.total)
   51|   order_square : ∀ z a,
   52|     (integral z a).hom.left ≫ (fibreIso z).hom.left ≫ Limits.pullback.fst _ _ =
   53|       (fibreIso z).hom.left ≫ Limits.pullback.fst _ _ ≫ (orderMap a).left

== CITE GeneralPELMember -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.GeneralTypeIII.GeneralPELMember
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32Native/GeneralTypeIIIDefs.lean  (lines 63–91)
   63| /-- Quaternionic presentation and the general-member condition in Lie vocabulary.
   64| The negative parameter makes the quaternion algebra totally definite over ℚ.
   65| The algebra equivalence retains the exact endomorphism algebra; `eisenstein` retains
   66| its selected quadratic subfield. `general` says the Hodge Lie algebra is the full
   67| polarization-skew centralizer of that algebra, rather than an arbitrary subalgebra.
   68| No Hodge generation or algebraicity conclusion is a field.
   69| Source: Abdulali, Annals of Mathematics 155 (2002), §2.3, Proposition 2.3.1,
   70| §3 and Theorem 4.1 (general member of a PEL-family of type III).
   71| The group-to-Lie dictionary is the repository convention, not a new comparison theorem. -/
   72| structure GeneralPELMember where
   73|   b : ℚ
   74|   b_neg : b < 0
   75|   end0 : ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b] ≃ₐ[ℚ] AbelianEnd0 A
   76|   eisenstein : W.J = (abelianEnd0Action A (end0 ⟨0, 1, 0, 0⟩)).unop
   77|   rosati : ∀ d x y,
   78|     polarizationForm (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) hM.nondegenerate
   79|       ((abelianEnd0Action A (end0 d)).unop x) y =
   80|     polarizationForm (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) hM.nondegenerate x
   81|       ((abelianEnd0Action A (end0 (star d))).unop y)
   82|   general :
   83|     let S := abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (finiteBetti_abelian A)
   84|       (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A) (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A)
   85|       (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A)
   86|     let E := polarizationForm (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) hM.nondegenerate
   87|     (hodgeLieAlgebra S.weight1.H S.weight1.isInternal : Set (Module.End ℚ (HQ A 1))) =
   88|       {X | (∀ x y, E (X x) y + E x (X y) = 0) ∧
   89|         ∀ d : AbelianEnd0 A, X.comp (abelianEnd0Action A d).unop =
   90|           (abelianEnd0Action A d).unop.comp X}

== CITE g3_power -> HodgeLean.Adapters.g3_power
FILE HodgeLean/Adapters/AbelianPowerDefs.lean  (lines 32–51)
   32| /-- **The power `A^r`**: the `r`-fold iterated cartesian product in `Over (Spec ℂ)`,
   33| `A^0 = 𝟙_ = Spec ℂ` and `A^(r+1) = A ⊗ A^r = A ×_ℂ A^r`.
   34| Source: Mumford, *Abelian Varieties*, §4 (products of abelian varieties); Mathlib
   35| `Over.cartesianMonoidalCategory`, `Over.tensorObj_left` (the tensor product in `Over X` is the
   36| fibre product over `X`). -/
   37| def g3_power : ℕ → Over (Spec ↧ℂ)
   38|   | 0 => 𝟙_ (Over (Spec ↧ℂ))
   39|   | n + 1 => A ⊗ g3_power n
   41| /-- `A^0` is the point `Spec ℂ` over itself. -/
   42| example : g3_power A 0 = Over.mk (𝟙 (Spec ↧ℂ)) := rfl
   44| /-- `A^(r+1)` is the fibre product `A ×_ℂ A^r`. -/
   45| example (n : ℕ) :
   46|     (g3_power A (n + 1)).left = Limits.pullback A.hom (g3_power A n).hom := rfl
   48| /-- **The group structure on `A^r`**: Mathlib's group objects `𝟙_` (`GrpObj.instTensorUnit`) and
   49| `G ⊗ H` (`GrpObj.tensorObj.instTensorObj`, componentwise), by recursion on `r`.
   50| Source: Mumford, *Abelian Varieties*, §4; Mathlib `GrpObj.instTensorUnit`,
   51| `GrpObj.tensorObj.instTensorObj`. -/

== CITE pelIsometryGroup -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.GeneralTypeIII.pelIsometryGroup
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32Native/PELIsometryGroup.lean  (lines 39–70)
   39| /-- The D-linear polarization isometries as a matrix algebraic group,
   40| constructed from their isometry and commutator equations.
   41| Source: Borel, Linear Algebraic Groups, second edition, §§1.6–1.7;
   42| Abdulali, Annals 155 (2002), §§3.1–3.3. -/
   43| def pelIsometryGroup (Ω : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)
   44|     (S : Set (Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)) : MatrixAlgGroup k n :=
   45|   g1_ofEquations
   46|     ({f | ∃ i j, f = g1_isometryEquations Ω i j} ∪
   47|       {f | ∃ T ∈ S, ∃ i j, f = g1_commEquation T i j})
   48|     (pelIsometrySubgroup Ω S) (by
   49|       intro g
   50|       constructor
   51|       · rintro ⟨hg, hc⟩ f (⟨i, j, rfl⟩ | ⟨T, hT, i, j, rfl⟩)
   52|         · rw [eval_isometry, hg, sub_self]; rfl
   53|         · rw [g1_eval_commEquation]
   54|           change ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k) * T - T * g) i j = 0
   55|           rw [(hc T hT).eq, sub_self]; rfl
   56|       · intro hg
   57|         constructor
   58|         · change (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k).transpose * Ω * g = Ω
   59|           apply sub_eq_zero.mp
   60|           ext i j
   61|           exact (eval_isometry Ω g i j) ▸ hg _ (Or.inl ⟨i, j, rfl⟩)
   62|         · intro T hT
   63|           apply sub_eq_zero.mp
   64|           ext i j
   65|           have h := hg _ (Or.inr ⟨T, hT, i, j, rfl⟩)
   66|           rw [g1_eval_commEquation] at h
   67|           exact h)
   69| /-- Membership retains both the polarization and every specified operator.
   70| Source: Borel, Linear Algebraic Groups, second edition, §§1.6–1.7. -/

== CITE ofTypeIII -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.GeneralTypeIII.PELSplitReference.ofTypeIII

   51| /-- The printed quaternionic form data supplies a reference with its actual integral order.

   53| def PELSplitReference.ofTypeIII (Q : TypeIIIFormData A pol) : PELSplitReference where
   54|   B := typeIIIReferenceVariety Q
   55|   polarized := typeIIIReference_polarized Q
   56|   b := Q.b
   57|   b_neg := Q.b_neg
   58|   end0 := Q.endomorphisms

   64|   full := actualEndOrder_full A Q.endomorphisms

== CITE Abdulali.typeIII_powers_assembly -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Abdulali.typeIII_powers_assembly
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Abdulali/TypeIIIPowersAssembly.lean  (lines 22–70)
   22|   (W : letI := (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A).finite
   23|     HodgeBlocks.WeilAction
   24|       (abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (finiteBetti_abelian A)
   25|         (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A) (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A)
   26|         (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A)).weight1 3)
   27|   (pol : HQ A 2)
   28|   (hM : IsMemberOfM (finiteBetti_abelian A) (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A)
   29|     (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A) (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) W pol)
   30| /-- The type-III powers implication for arbitrary geometric biproduct powers,
   31| retaining the selected rational quaternionic degree-six space.
   32| Source: Abdulali, Annals of Mathematics 155 (2002), Remark 4.2 and proof of
   33| Theorem 4.1, p. 924; Weyl, The Classical Groups, II.9 and II.17. -/
   34| theorem typeIII_powers_assembly :
   35|     ∀ (P : GeneralPELMember A W pol hM),
   36|       (∀ x : HQ A (2*3), x ∈ quaternionicSpan A (weilSix A W pol hM) →
   37|         x ∈ algebraicCycleClassSpan A 3) →
   38|       ∀ (Y : Over (Spec ↧ℂ)) [GrpObj Y] [IsCommMonObj Y]
   39|         [IsIntegral Y.left] [Smooth Y.hom] [IsProjective Y.hom]
   40|         (r : ℕ), 0 < r → AbelianPowerData A Y r →
   41|         ∀ p : ℕ, Hdg^p(Y; ℚ) ≤ algebraicCycleClassSpan Y p := by
   42|   intro P hQ Y _ _ _ _ _ r hr power p
   43|   by_cases hp : p = 0
   44|   · subst p
   45|     exact power_hodge_degree_zero (Y := Y)
   46|   by_cases hd : r * TopologicalSpace.dim A.left < p
   47|   · exact power_hodge_above_dimension A power p hd
   48|   obtain ⟨s, a, e, B, hs, ha, hI, hsym, hB, hJ, he, hform, hc⟩ :=
   49|     typeIII_skew_centralizer_normal_form A W pol hM P
   50|   apply (Submodule.baseChange_le_iff (A := ℂ)).mp
   51|   intro z hz
   52|   let E := (exteriorEquiv (2*p) (splitPowerH1Equiv power e)).trans (complexCupEquiv Y (2*p))
   53|   let w := E.symm z
   54|   have hEz : complexCupEquiv Y (2*p)
   55|       (exteriorPower.map (2*p) (splitPowerH1Equiv power e).toLinearMap w) = z :=
   56|     E.apply_symm_apply z
   57|   have hw : w ∈ (m1b_fixed (m1b_copies (r*2) ''
   58|       {u ∈ m1b_isometries B | u.det = 1}) (2*p)).map
   59|         (InvariantsExterior.wedgeQuotient (2*p)) := by
   60|     apply (split_power_hodge_iff A e B hB hsym hc power p w).mp
   61|     rw [hEz]
   62|     exact hz
   63|   obtain ⟨t, ht, htw⟩ := Submodule.mem_map.mp hw
   64|   have halg := orthogonal_tensor_images_algebraic Y (r*2) B hB hsym.eq
   65|     (splitPowerH1Equiv power e).toLinearMap
   66|     (split_power_quadratics_algebraic A e B hB hsym hc power)
   67|     (split_power_volumes_algebraic A W pol hM P hQ s hs a hI e he power) p t ht
   68|   rw [htw, hEz] at halg
   69|   exact halg

== CITE TypeIIIPaper.generalPELMember_of_generic_hodge_group -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.TypeIIIPaper.generalPELMember_of_generic_hodge_group
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32Native/G32PELGenericMember.lean  (lines 23–68)
   23| /-- The generic Hodge group of the connected quaternionic PEL isometry
   24|  group supplies the original G32 general-member record. The input is an
   25|  algebraic group equality, and the rational Lie equality is derived internally.
   26|  Source: Abdulali, Annals of Mathematics 155 (2002), Proposition 2.3.1,
   27|  pp. 919, 922, and §§3.1–3.3; Borel, Linear Algebraic Groups, §3.5. -/
   28| def generalPELMember_of_generic_hodge_group
   29|     (b : ℚ) (hb : b < 0) (end0 : ℍ[ℚ, -3, 0, b] ≃ₐ[ℚ] AbelianEnd0 A)
   30|     (heisenstein : W.J = (abelianEnd0Action A (end0 ⟨0, 1, 0, 0⟩)).unop)
   31|     (hadj : ∀ d x y,
   32|       polarizationForm (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) h.nondegenerate
   33|         ((abelianEnd0Action A (end0 d)).unop x) y =
   34|       polarizationForm (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) h.nondegenerate x
   35|         ((abelianEnd0Action A (end0 (star d))).unop y))
   36|     {n : ℕ} (basis : Module.Basis (Fin n) ℚ (HQ A 1))
   37|     (hGroup : g2_hodgeGroup
   38|       (abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (finiteBetti_abelian A)
   39|         (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A) (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A)
   40|         (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A)).weight1.H
   41|       (abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (finiteBetti_abelian A)
   42|         (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A) (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A)
   43|         (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A)).weight1.isInternal basis =
   44|       GeneralTypeIII.pelGenericGroup
   45|         (LinearMap.toMatrix₂ (Algebra.TensorProduct.basis ℂ basis)
   46|           (Algebra.TensorProduct.basis ℂ basis)
   47|           ((polarizationForm (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) h.nondegenerate).baseChange ℂ))
   48|         (Set.range fun i : Fin 4 =>
   49|           LinearMap.toMatrix (Algebra.TensorProduct.basis ℂ basis)
   50|             (Algebra.TensorProduct.basis ℂ basis)
   51|             ((abelianEnd0Action A
   52|               (end0 (QuaternionAlgebra.basisOneIJK (-3 : ℚ) 0 b i))).unop.baseChange ℂ))) :
   53|     GeneralPELMember A W pol h := by
   54|   let P := GeneralTypeIII.generalPELMember_of_isometries A W pol
   55|     (GeneralTypeIII.SixfoldData.ofMember A W pol h)
   56|     b hb end0 heisenstein hadj basis hGroup
   57|   exact {
   58|     b := P.b
   59|     b_neg := P.b_neg
   60|     end0 := P.end0
   61|     eisenstein := P.eisenstein
   62|     rosati := P.rosati
   63|     general := P.general }
   65| /-- A member with the generic PEL Hodge group satisfies the exact rational
   66| Lie condition used by the G32 powers implication.
   67| Source: Abdulali, Annals of Mathematics 155 (2002), Proposition 2.3.1,
   68| p. 919, and §4, p. 922; Borel, Linear Algebraic Groups, §3.5. -/

== CITE GeneralTypeIII.generalPELMember_of_isometries -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.GeneralTypeIII.generalPELMember_of_isometries

   45|           ((polarizationForm (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) h.nondegenerate).baseChange ℂ))

   52|   generalPELMember_of_group A W pol h b hb end0 heisenstein hadj basis _ hGroup

== CITE PELSplitFamily.g32_generalPELMember_of_family -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.GeneralTypeIII.PELSplitFamily.g32_generalPELMember_of_family
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32Native/G32PELFamilyGenericMember.lean  (lines 43–86)
   43| /-- A general member of the retained effective polarized PEL family supplies
   44| the original G32 record. Exactness of End⁰ and the rational Lie condition
   45| are derived, rather than included in the generic-member premise.
   46| Source: Abdulali, Annals of Mathematics 155 (2002), Proposition 2.3.1,
   47| p. 919, and §4, p. 922. -/
   48| def g32_generalPELMember_of_family (z : PELSplitComponent R)
   49|     (hgeneral : F.hodgeGroup z = F.genericGroup)
   50|     (h : IsMemberOfM (finiteBetti_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   51|       (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   52|       (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   53|       (torusCohomology_cupProductOf (F.fibre z).X) (F.fibre z).W (F.fibre z).pol) :
   54|     TypeIIIPaper.GeneralPELMember (F.fibre z).X (F.fibre z).W (F.fibre z).pol h := by
   55|   let end0 := AlgEquiv.ofBijective (F.action z) (F.action_bijective_of_general z hgeneral)
   56|   apply TypeIIIPaper.generalPELMember_of_generic_hodge_group
   57|     (F.fibre z).X (F.fibre z).W (F.fibre z).pol h R.b R.b_neg end0
   58|     (F.action_generator z) (F.action_adjoint z) (F.basis z)
   59|   have hH : (abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (finiteBetti_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   60|       (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   61|       (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   62|       (torusCohomology_cupProductOf (F.fibre z).X)).weight1.H =
   63|       latticeHodgePieces (F.fibre z).X := spec_g4_cohomologyHodgePiece_one_eq _
   64|   have hg : g2_hodgeGroup
   65|       (abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (finiteBetti_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   66|         (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   67|         (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   68|         (torusCohomology_cupProductOf (F.fibre z).X)).weight1.H
   69|       (abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (finiteBetti_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   70|         (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   71|         (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian (F.fibre z).X)
   72|         (torusCohomology_cupProductOf (F.fibre z).X)).weight1.isInternal (F.basis z) =
   73|       F.hodgeGroup z := by
   74|     congr 1
   75|   rw [hg, F.form_matrix z]
   76|   change F.hodgeGroup z = pelGenericGroup _
   77|     (Set.range fun i : Fin 4 => LinearMap.toMatrix _ _
   78|       ((abelianEnd0Action (F.fibre z).X
   79|         (F.action z (QuaternionAlgebra.basisOneIJK (-3 : ℚ) 0 R.b i))).unop.baseChange ℂ))
   80|   simp only [LinearMap.toMatrix_baseChange, F.action_matrix]
   81|   exact pel_general_member_hodge_group R F z hgeneral
   83| /-- The literal rational Lie equality required by G32's powers argument,
   84| from a general member of the retained effective PEL family.
   85| Source: Abdulali, Annals of Mathematics 155 (2002), Proposition 2.3.1,
   86| p. 919, and §4, p. 922. -/

== CITE typeIII_powers_assembly -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Abdulali.typeIII_powers_assembly
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Abdulali/TypeIIIPowersAssembly.lean  (lines 22–70)
   22|   (W : letI := (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A).finite
   23|     HodgeBlocks.WeilAction
   24|       (abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (finiteBetti_abelian A)
   25|         (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A) (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A)
   26|         (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A)).weight1 3)
   27|   (pol : HQ A 2)
   28|   (hM : IsMemberOfM (finiteBetti_abelian A) (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A)
   29|     (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A) (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) W pol)
   30| /-- The type-III powers implication for arbitrary geometric biproduct powers,
   31| retaining the selected rational quaternionic degree-six space.
   32| Source: Abdulali, Annals of Mathematics 155 (2002), Remark 4.2 and proof of
   33| Theorem 4.1, p. 924; Weyl, The Classical Groups, II.9 and II.17. -/
   34| theorem typeIII_powers_assembly :
   35|     ∀ (P : GeneralPELMember A W pol hM),
   36|       (∀ x : HQ A (2*3), x ∈ quaternionicSpan A (weilSix A W pol hM) →
   37|         x ∈ algebraicCycleClassSpan A 3) →
   38|       ∀ (Y : Over (Spec ↧ℂ)) [GrpObj Y] [IsCommMonObj Y]
   39|         [IsIntegral Y.left] [Smooth Y.hom] [IsProjective Y.hom]
   40|         (r : ℕ), 0 < r → AbelianPowerData A Y r →
   41|         ∀ p : ℕ, Hdg^p(Y; ℚ) ≤ algebraicCycleClassSpan Y p := by
   42|   intro P hQ Y _ _ _ _ _ r hr power p
   43|   by_cases hp : p = 0
   44|   · subst p
   45|     exact power_hodge_degree_zero (Y := Y)
   46|   by_cases hd : r * TopologicalSpace.dim A.left < p
   47|   · exact power_hodge_above_dimension A power p hd
   48|   obtain ⟨s, a, e, B, hs, ha, hI, hsym, hB, hJ, he, hform, hc⟩ :=
   49|     typeIII_skew_centralizer_normal_form A W pol hM P
   50|   apply (Submodule.baseChange_le_iff (A := ℂ)).mp
   51|   intro z hz
   52|   let E := (exteriorEquiv (2*p) (splitPowerH1Equiv power e)).trans (complexCupEquiv Y (2*p))
   53|   let w := E.symm z
   54|   have hEz : complexCupEquiv Y (2*p)
   55|       (exteriorPower.map (2*p) (splitPowerH1Equiv power e).toLinearMap w) = z :=
   56|     E.apply_symm_apply z
   57|   have hw : w ∈ (m1b_fixed (m1b_copies (r*2) ''
   58|       {u ∈ m1b_isometries B | u.det = 1}) (2*p)).map
   59|         (InvariantsExterior.wedgeQuotient (2*p)) := by
   60|     apply (split_power_hodge_iff A e B hB hsym hc power p w).mp
   61|     rw [hEz]
   62|     exact hz
   63|   obtain ⟨t, ht, htw⟩ := Submodule.mem_map.mp hw
   64|   have halg := orthogonal_tensor_images_algebraic Y (r*2) B hB hsym.eq
   65|     (splitPowerH1Equiv power e).toLinearMap
   66|     (split_power_quadratics_algebraic A e B hB hsym hc power)
   67|     (split_power_volumes_algebraic A W pol hM P hQ s hs a hI e he power) p t ht
   68|   rw [htw, hEz] at halg
   69|   exact halg

== CITE TypeIIIPaper.powers_of_weil_algebraic -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.TypeIIIPaper.powers_of_weil_algebraic

   11| /-- The Weil-to-powers implication with the powers theorem supplied as `hPowers`.
   12| Source: Abdulali, Hodge structures on abelian varieties of type III,
   13| Annals of Mathematics 155 (2002), 915–928, Remark 4.2 and proof of Theorem 4.1,

   17|     [GrpObj A] [IsCommMonObj A] [IsIntegral A.left] [Smooth A.hom] [IsProjective A.hom]

   19|       HodgeBlocks.WeilAction

   27|     (hPowers : ∀ (P : GeneralPELMember A W pol hMember),
   28|       (∀ x : HQ A (2 * 3), x ∈ quaternionicSpan A (weilSix A W pol hMember) →
   29|         x ∈ algebraicCycleClassSpan A 3) →
   30|       ∀ (Y : Over (Spec ↧ℂ)) [GrpObj Y] [IsCommMonObj Y]

   34|     (hWeil : WeilClassesAlgebraic (finiteBetti_abelian A) (spec_bettiDeRhamIso_abelian A)
   35|       (hodgeDecomposition_of_abelian A) (torusCohomology_cupProductOf A) W
   36|       hMember.isWeilType)
   37|     (Y : Over (Spec ↧ℂ)) [GrpObj Y] [IsCommMonObj Y]
   38|     [IsIntegral Y.left] [Smooth Y.hom] [IsProjective Y.hom]
   39|     (r : ℕ) (hr : 0 < r) (power : AbelianPowerData A Y r) :
   40|     ∀ p : ℕ, Hdg^p(Y; ℚ) ≤ algebraicCycleClassSpan Y p := by
   41|   exact hPowers P (quaternionic_weil_algebraic A W pol hMember hWeil) Y r hr power

   38| set_option genInjectivity false in
   39| set_option genSizeOfSpec false in
   40| /-- The remaining normal-field and paired numerical inputs on the selected covers.
   41| Source: Lee, Introduction to Smooth Manifolds, second edition, Theorem 6.24;
   42| Schoen, Compositio Mathematica 65 (1988), Sublemma 2.7. -/
   43| structure G32RemainingGeometricInputs {c : Fin 10 → ℂ} {g : Fin 13 → b4b_Group}
   44|     (D : b65z_SharedFamily c g)
   45|     (hg : g32h_Nielsen (fun j ↦ b4b_toMatrix (g j)))
   46|     (B : Module.Basis (Fin 12) ℤ
   47|       (b63l_parameterLattice c D.C D.hC (b6t_polydisc c) (Set.Subset.refl _) (b65z_s₀ c))) where
   48|   /-- The original analytic displacement on the dual conormal.
   49|   Source: Lee, Introduction to Smooth Manifolds, second edition, Theorem 6.24. -/
   50|   retained_euclidean_normal_field : ∀ {Q W : Over (Spec ↧ℂ)} [IsIntegral Q.left]
   51|       [LocallyOfFiniteType Q.hom] [QuasiCompact Q.hom] [IsSeparated Q.hom]
   52|       [LocallyOfFiniteType W.hom] [QuasiCompact W.hom] [IsSeparated W.hom]
   53|       (inc : Q ⟶ W) [IsClosedImmersion inc.left] (k : ℕ)
   54|       {V : Scheme} (v : V ⟶ W.left) [IsOpenImmersion v]
   55|       (j : Q.left ⟶ V) [IsClosedImmersion j] (hj : j ≫ v = inc.left)
   56|       [SmoothOfRelativeDimension k Q.hom]
   57|       [SmoothOfRelativeDimension (k + k) (v ≫ W.hom)],
   58|     letI : IsClosedImmersion (i1_smoothLift inc v j hj).left := by
   59|       change IsClosedImmersion j
   60|       infer_instance
   61|     letI : SmoothOfRelativeDimension (k + k) (Over.mk (v ≫ W.hom)).hom :=
   62|       ‹SmoothOfRelativeDimension (k + k) (v ≫ W.hom)›
   63|     ∀ (hD : ∀ (A : V.Opens) (hA : IsAffineOpen A) (x : ComplexPoint Q)
   64|       (hx : x ∈ Point.overOpen (j ⁻¹ᵁ A)) (z : ComplexPoint (Over.mk (v ≫ W.hom)))
   65|       (hz : Point.map (i1_smoothLift inc v j hj) x ∈ (chartAt (Fin (k + k) → ℂ) z).source),
   66|       Function.Injective (retainedAffineConormalDifferential (Over.mk (v ≫ W.hom)) Q (i1_smoothLift inc v j hj) (k + k) A hA x hx z hz) ∧
   67|       Module.finrank ℂ (LinearMap.range
   68|         (retainedAffineConormalDifferential (Over.mk (v ≫ W.hom)) Q (i1_smoothLift inc v j hj) (k + k) A hA x hx z hz)) = k)
   69|     (g : ChartHermitianMetric (ComplexPoint (Over.mk (v ≫ W.hom))) (k + k)) (n : ℕ)
   70|     (emb : ComplexPoint (Over.mk (v ≫ W.hom)) → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n))
   71|     (hemb : ContMDiff 𝓘(ℝ, Fin (k + k) → ℂ) 𝓘(ℝ, EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) ω emb),
   72|     Nonempty (RetainedEuclideanNormalField inc k v j hj n emb g)
   73|   /-- Numerical nonvanishing on the very component retained by the joint construction.
   74|   Source: Schoen, Compositio Mathematica 65 (1988), Sublemma 2.7. -/
   75|   paired_self_intersection :
   76|     ∀ (t : b6t_polydisc c) (S : g32p_Supplier)
   77|       (hA : g32p_Admissible (Classical.choose (g32y_cover_spec D t hg B)).aut S)
   78|       (hNe : g32p_exponents S ≠ []),
   79|       let R := Classical.choose (jointSelectedPrefix_spec D t hg B S hA)
   80|       let P := jointSelectedOrientedPositiveData (@g32l4_paired_blowup_regularity_aux) R hNe
   81|       ∃ degree : Cohomology ℚ (TopCat.of (ComplexPoint P.C.Q)) (2 * R.P.k) →ₗ[ℚ] ℚ,
   82|         degree (s2e_normalEuler P.C.ι R.P.k P.c) ≠ 0

== CITE IsSimpleExps -> HodgeLean.Papers.IsSimpleExps
FILE HodgeLean/Papers/PapersArithmeticDefs.lean  (lines 296–306)
  296| /-- Schoen's simplicity: after reordering, `α_{2i} = −α_{2i−1}` in `ℤ/m` for all `i`, i.e. the
  297| exponents can be split into pairs with sum `≡ 0 (mod m)`.
  298| Source: G32 v0.16, Subsection 4.1 (following Schoen, *Hodge classes on self-products of a variety
  299| with an automorphism*, Compositio 65 (1988), §1). -/
  300| def IsSimpleExps (m : ℕ) (α : List ℕ) : Prop :=
  301|   ∃ L : List (ℕ × ℕ), (L.flatMap fun p => [p.1, p.2]).Perm α ∧ ∀ p ∈ L, (p.1 + p.2) % m = 0
  303| example : IsSimpleExps 3 [1, 2] := ⟨[(1, 2)], List.Perm.refl _, by decide⟩
  305| /-- The greatest common divisor of `m` and the exponents (column `d` of G32 Table 1).
  306| Source: G32 v0.16, Appendix B ("`d = gcd(m, α)` for ramified rows"). -/

  222|         let γ := s2e_rationalOrbitCycle ι d₀ S.m (a j)
  223|         (∀ x ∈ γ.support, Order.coheight x = P.k) ∧
  224|         ∃ cj, a3b4_IsCycleClass W₀.hom γ P.k cj ∧
  225|           AlgebraicTopology.Singular.relativeCohomologyToAbsolute ℚ _ _ cj = v j) ∧
  226|       ∀ ζ : ℂ, IsPrimitiveRoot ζ S.m →
  227|         (∃ u : ℂ ⊗[ℚ] H₀, u ≠ 0 ∧
  228|           (s2e_pullback ℚ d₀ (2 * P.k)).baseChange ℂ u = ζ • u) ∧
  229|         (∀ u : ℂ ⊗[ℚ] H₀,
  230|           (s2e_pullback ℚ d₀ (2 * P.k)).baseChange ℂ u = ζ • u →
  231|           ∃ b : Fin (Nat.totient S.m) → ℂ,
  232|             u = ∑ j, b j • TensorProduct.tmul ℚ (1 : ℂ) (v j)) := by

== CITE g32p_SchoenSupplier -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.g32p_SchoenSupplier
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32PropFourSixDefs.lean  (lines 366–390)
  366| /-- **Schoen's supplier conclusion**, for the primitive parts of the admissible suppliers of a
  367| cover: every cyclotomic determinant class is algebraic. The first field is Schoen's theorem where
  368| his Sublemma 2.9 applies; the second is the remaining case (rows 13–15 among the fifty-one),
  369| where that step is replaced by G32 Lemma 4.3.
  370| Source: C. Schoen, *Hodge classes on self-products of a variety with an automorphism*, Compositio
  371| Math. 65 (1988), 3–32, Theorem 2.0 and Corollary 3.1; G32 v0.16, Lemmas 4.2 and 4.3. -/
  372| structure g32p_SchoenSupplier {A : g32t_Sixfold} (X : g32p_Cover A) : Prop where
  373|   schoen : ∀ S (hS : g32p_Admissible X.aut S), g32p_PrimeAvailable S →
  374|     cyc_Algebraic (cupProductOf (X.part S hS).P) (X.part S hS).act.cyc_coordinates
  375|   withoutPrime : ∀ S (hS : g32p_Admissible X.aut S), ¬ g32p_PrimeAvailable S →
  376|     cyc_Algebraic (cupProductOf (X.part S hS).P) (X.part S hS).act.cyc_coordinates
  378| example {A : g32t_Sixfold} (X : g32p_Cover A) (h : g32p_SchoenSupplier X) (S : g32p_Supplier)
  379|     (hS : g32p_Admissible X.aut S) :
  380|     cyc_Algebraic (cupProductOf (X.part S hS).P) (X.part S hS).act.cyc_coordinates := by
  381|   by_cases hE : g32p_PrimeAvailable S
  382|   · exact h.schoen S hS hE
  383|   · exact h.withoutPrime S hS hE
  385| /-- The complex points of Milne's group `S(J)`: invertible `ℂ`-linear maps of `H¹(J; ℂ)`
  386| commuting with the pullback of every endomorphism and fixing the polarization in
  387| `⋀² H¹(J; ℂ)`.
  388| Source: J. S. Milne, *Lefschetz classes on abelian varieties*, Duke Math. J. 96 (1999), §1
  389| (`S(A)`, the largest algebraic subgroup of `Sp` commuting with `End⁰(A)`); G32 v0.16, proof of
  390| Proposition 4.6. -/

== CITE g32p_PrimeAvailable -> HodgeLean.Foundations.Program.g32p_PrimeAvailable
FILE HodgeLean/Foundations/Program/G32PropFourSixDefs.lean  (lines 195–209)
  195| /-- The case where Schoen's Sublemma 2.9 applies: every exponent `α` has a prime `p ∣ m` with
  196| `p ∤ α`. Otherwise (for instance `m = 12` with every exponent `6`, rows 13–15) the prime it
  197| chooses does not exist, and G32 Lemma 4.3 replaces that step.
  198| Source: Schoen (1988), Sublemma 2.9, p. 19; G32 v0.16, Lemma 4.3 and the paragraph before it. -/
  199| def g32p_PrimeAvailable (S : g32p_Supplier) : Prop :=
  200|   ∀ a ∈ g32p_exponents S, ∃ p, p.Prime ∧ p ∣ S.m ∧ ¬ p ∣ a
  202| example (S : g32p_Supplier) (h : g32p_PrimeAvailable S) (a : ℕ) (ha : a ∈ g32p_exponents S) :
  203|     ∃ p, p.Prime ∧ p ∣ S.m ∧ ¬ p ∣ a := h a ha
  205| /-! ## Cohomology of a variety with a `G`-action -/
  207| /-- The multiplicity `⟨χ_{H¹}, χ_τ⟩` of a complex representation `τ` in `H¹(J; ℂ)`, for `G`
  208| acting on `J` through `aut`, computed from the traces of the pullbacks.
  209| Source: Serre, *Linear representations of finite groups*, §2.3, Theorem 4. -/

== CITE abelianVarietyCohomologyOf -> HodgeLean.RealDefs.abelianVarietyCohomologyOf
FILE HodgeLean/RealDefs/AbelianVariety.lean  (lines 346–368)
  346| **Referee disclosure of `AbelianVarietyCohomology`.** That docstring says `weight1` and
  347| `cohomology` are independent data, and that `cohomology m` need not be the Hodge structure induced
  348| on `⋀^m` of `weight1`. This package answers the half about provenance: `weight1` and every
  349| `cohomology m` are the Hodge structures of the real cohomology of one `X`, and `rank_eq` holds with
  350| `g = dim X.left`. It does **not** answer the half about compatibility: no theorem here shows that
  351| `cohomology m` equals `weight1.exteriorPower m` (the roadmap's `IsExteriorOfWeightOne`). That needs
  352| `cupWedgeMap` to be a morphism of Hodge structures (from O5, `CupFiltered`). It is proved, under
  353| C1, C2, C4, C7 and O5, as `isExteriorOfWeightOne_of_cupFiltered` in `AbelianExterior.lean`. -/
  354| noncomputable def abelianVarietyCohomologyOf (hF : FiniteBetti X) (hB : BettiDeRhamIso X)
  355|     (hD : HodgeDecomposition X) (hT : TorusCohomology μ) :
  356|     letI := hT.finite
  357|     HodgeBlocks.AbelianVarietyCohomology (HQ X 1) :=
  358|   letI := hT.finite
  359|   { g := dim X.left
  360|     rank_eq := hT.finrank_eq
  361|     weight1 := cohomologyHodgeStructure hF hB hD 1
  362|     cohomology := fun m =>
  363|       letI := hF.finite m
  364|       HodgeBlocks.QHodgeStructure.congr (HQ X m) (cohomologyHodgeStructure hF hB hD m)
  365|         (hT.cupWedgeEquiv m).symm }
  367| /-- A class `v ∈ ⋀^(2p) H¹` is a Hodge class of the package's `cohomology (2p)` exactly when its
  368| cup product `cupWedgeMap (2p) v` lies in upstream's `Hdg^p(X; ℚ)`. -/

== CITE cohomologyHodgeStructure -> HodgeLean.RealDefs.cohomologyHodgeStructure
FILE HodgeLean/RealDefs/HodgeStructure.lean  (lines 236–249)
  236| Checks: F-1666 -/
  237| noncomputable def cohomologyHodgeStructure (hF : FiniteBetti X) (hB : BettiDeRhamIso X)
  238|     (hD : HodgeDecomposition X) (n : ℕ) :
  239|     letI := hF.finite n
  240|     HodgeBlocks.QHodgeStructure (n : ℤ) (HQ X n) :=
  241|   letI := hF.finite n
  242|   { H := cohomologyHodgePiece X n
  243|     supported := fun pq h => by simp only [cohomologyHodgePiece, h, ↓reduceIte]
  244|     isInternal := by
  245|       rw [cohomologyHodgePiece_eq_map hB n]
  246|       exact (hD.isInternal n).mapEquiv _
  247|     conj_symm := cohomologyHodgePiece_map_sigmaQ X n }
  249| /-- The `(p, q)` piece of `cohomologyHodgeStructure` is `cohomologyHodgePiece`. -/

   18| **Honest disclosure (referee finding, hodge-LEAN17):** this structure does NOT enforce, by any
   19| field, that `cohomology m` is literally the Hodge structure induced from `weight1`'s `m`-th
   20| wedge power in the sense of Theorem 1.4.1 above -- doing so would need the base-change/exterior-
   21| power construction that `HodgeBlocks/HodgeStructure/ExteriorPower.lean` explicitly defers, and
   22| is not attempted here. `weight1` and `cohomology` are independent data; an instantiation is
   23| faithful to an actual abelian variety only when the caller supplies fields that really do satisfy
   24| this compatibility, which nothing in this file checks or proves. `rank_eq` and `weight1` are, in
   25| particular, not used by any theorem below. -/
   26| @[hodge_hypothesis]
   27| structure AbelianVarietyCohomology where
   28|   g : ℕ
   29|   rank_eq : Module.finrank ℚ V = 2 * g
   30|   weight1 : QHodgeStructure 1 V
   31|   cohomology : (m : ℕ) → QHodgeStructure (m : ℤ) (⋀[ℚ]^m V)
   33| variable {V}
   35| /-- **The milestone statement.** Every Hodge class in `Λ^{2p} V` (standing for `H^{2p}(A,ℚ)`) is
   36| algebraic, for a chosen algebraicity predicate `IsAlgebraic`. -/

== CITE FiniteBetti -> HodgeLean.RealDefs.FiniteBetti
FILE HodgeLean/RealDefs/HodgeStructure.lean  (lines 171–195)
  171| **Consistency.** For `X = Spec ℂ` (as `𝟙 : Spec ℂ ⟶ Spec ℂ`), `X(ℂ)` is one point, so
  172| `H^0(X; ℚ) = ℚ` and `H^n = 0` for `n > 0`, because global sections are exact on a point; each is
  173| finite-dimensional. This is not proved here for upstream's `Sheaf.H`. -/
  174| @[hodge_hypothesis]
  175| structure FiniteBetti [IsProjective X.hom] : Prop where
  176|   finite : ∀ n : ℕ, Module.Finite ℚ (HQ X n)
  178| /-- Betti–de Rham comparison: the `ℂ`-linear extension `ℂ ⊗[ℚ] H^n(X(ℂ); ℚ) → H_dR^n(X)` of
  179| upstream's coefficient map is bijective.
  181| Source: Hatcher, *Algebraic Topology*, §3.1 (the universal coefficient theorem for cohomology), with Bredon, *Sheaf Theory*, 2nd ed., GTM 170, Ch. III (sheaf cohomology with constant coefficients on a manifold is singular cohomology); sections only.
  183| Upstream already proves the holomorphic Poincaré lemma (`complexConstantCohomologyDeRhamEquiv`), so
  184| the content is the change of coefficients `ℂ ⊗[ℚ] H^n(X(ℂ); ℚ) ≅ H^n(X(ℂ); ℂ)`. For the compact
  185| manifold `X(ℂ)` the integral homology is finitely generated, and the universal coefficient theorem
  186| with `Ext(-, ℂ) = 0` gives the isomorphism. The structure also takes as given that the map is the
  187| one induced by `ℚ → ℂ` on constant sheaves, which is how upstream defines it.
  189| **Consistency.** For `X = Spec ℂ`, in degree `0` both sides are `ℂ` and `z ⊗ 1 ↦ z · 1`; in degree
  190| `n > 0` both sides are `0`. This is expected, not proved here.
  192| **Non-degeneracy.** At `n = 0`, the source contains `1 ⊗ [X]` with `[X] ≠ 0`
  193| (`cycleComponentSheafClass_genericPoint_ne_zero`), and `ℂ ⊗[ℚ] -` is faithful over a field, so a
  194| bijection forces `H_dR^0(X) ≠ 0`. -/
  195| @[hodge_hypothesis]

   14| full order acting by actual endomorphisms, and its compatible polarization.
   15| The lattice used below is this variety's geometric lattice.
   16| Source: G34 v1.5, Corollary 12.1; Birkenhake–Lange, §9.6. -/

   34|   integral_eq : ∀ a, (1 : ℚ) ⊗ₜ[ℤ] integral a = end0 a.val

== CITE hodgeClasses -> AlgebraicGeometry.ComplexPoint.hodgeClasses
FILE .lake/packages/HodgeConjecture/HodgeConjecture/Definitions/AlgebraicGeometry/Hodge/Filtration.lean  (lines 911–927)
  911| /-- Let `X` be a smooth integral scheme over `ℂ`, and give `X(ℂ)` its analytic topology. For a field
  912| `K` embedded in `ℂ` and a natural number `p`, these are the classes in `H^{2p}(X(ℂ); K)` whose
  913| de Rham images belong to `F^p ∩ conjugate(F^p)`. The filtration `F^p` is the image of the
  914| hypercohomology of holomorphic forms of degrees at least `p`. Conjugation is induced by
  915| conjugating constant complex coefficients; for projective `X`, the intersection is the Hodge
  916| component of type `(p,p)`. -/
  917| def hodgeClasses [IsIntegral X.left] [Smooth X.hom] (p : ℕ) :
  918|     Submodule K (H^(2 * p)(X; K)) :=
  919|   ((hodgePiece X p p (2 * p : ℕ)).restrictScalars K).comap
  920|     (fieldToDeRhamCohomologyLinear K X (2 * p))
  922| /-- `Hdg^p(f; K)` is the space of Hodge classes of codimension `p` with coefficients in `K`.
  924| The literature writes `Hdg^p(X.left)` for the variety `X.left` alone; here the variety is
  925| presented by its structure morphism `f`, and the coefficient field is named. -/
  926| scoped notation:max "Hdg^" p:max "(" f "; " K ")" => hodgeClasses K f p
